# Per-cell attribution of the blown histogram — which object actually owns each white pixel.
#
# Produced by tools/clip-attribution-probe.mjs. This is the sibling of
# tools/cdp-clip-attribution.mjs (committed at 8488ae4, log clip-attribution-2026-09-27.log), which
# ablates the post chain's *gates*; this one raycasts the 160x100 sample cell by cell and names the
# object behind each blown cell. Both were run the same morning on the same two tabs.
#
# SIDES. "HEAD side" = a byte-identical mirror of commit c37fdbb served from /tmp/rsb-head-srv on
# :5174 (git archive of src + symlinks for index.html/vendor/public/assets). "working-tree side" =
# this checkout on :5173. sha256 of the five files that differ, first 16 hex digits:
#   src/world/assets.js        HEAD 908373ac278e9978   worktree d924071060ad13bb
#   src/world/sky.js           HEAD 7e9ac68a38c26a28   worktree aee116c669a77b24
#   src/world/environment.js   HEAD 5ab1bebc5b3f8b06   worktree 8bf9a265ff328966
#   src/main.js                HEAD 45b7fecd1d342d1f   worktree e2c4fde176d8de67
#   src/world/props.js         HEAD 30134d0aa627d600   worktree dcf9b73f0b68f8dd
# (Refreshed 11:07 +08:00, after the comment edits that folded the run counts into src/. The mirror's
# src files were checked equal to `git show c37fdbb:<file>` before the first run, and they still are.
# Every run below is anchored to the CODE, not to these file hashes, because comments were still being
# written while the passes were running: the code-only digest of the three changed files, computed by
# tools/code-digest.mjs (drop comment lines, drop blank lines, sha256 of the rest), is
#   src/world/assets.js       108 code lines  aed491bbc9eb70ad
#   src/world/sky.js          154 code lines  e54fee8b6cd44c0f
#   src/world/environment.js  137 code lines  3bd95be3a971b591
# and it is the same before and after the last comment edits. That digester has both controls run on
# it: appending a whole comment line to sky.js leaves the digest at e54fee8b6cd44c0f, and changing one
# digit inside the shader (0.99985 -> 0.99986) moves it to 983846b8f337094b. The code lines themselves
# are in the NON-COMMENT DIFF appendix at the bottom of this file, so a reader can check that the trio
# this attribution measured is the trio that shipped.) Of the
# five worktree diffs, main.js and props.js are comment-only (the #105 census markers and a wrong
# offset in one of them), so they cannot move a pixel; the three that can are assets.js, sky.js
# and environment.js — the trio this attribution was gathered for.
#
# UNITS. clip = % of the 16 000-px (160x100) sample at luminance >= 224, the sweep's own ruler
# (src/main.js:5084-5095, 5158-5165). bins = per-mille. "cells" = histogram pixels, one each.
# The dome's cell count is a LOWER bound: the sky sphere draws with depthWrite:false, so a cell where
# some geometry is nearer is credited to that geometry even where the sky is what painted it.
#
# BUFFER. Every run prints renderer.domElement.width/height, i.e. the DRAWING buffer, and the sweep
# prints the same field (tools/cdp-clip-sweep.mjs:105). It is 1810x740 or 1303x532 depending on
# whether the game's auto-degrade has fired in that tab: main.js:2657-2663 drops renderCap to 0.72
# after 8 s of fpsAvg < 30, and 1810 x 0.72 = 1303. So the two numbers are the same window at two
# different caps, not two windows — and the runs below are labelled with the cap they ran at. The
# clip/blown figures move a little between them (HEAD pad:motor reads 1.7 at 0.72 and 1.5 at 1.0),
# which is why each claim in src/ cites a range and this file, not a single row.
